# Oscill .ofw PLAINTEXT RECONSTRUCTION  (decrypted where the mask is known)
#
# Basis: docs/ofw_mask_cryptanalysis.spike.md + docs/ofw_mask_solver.* (C_OMS) — additive
#   keystream C=(P+K)%256, period 514.
#   Mask now 111/514 (21.6%): 64 lattice (body offset==4 mod8, 0xFF) + K[1] SEQ + K[2] reset opcode
#     + reset LJMP target cols 3/4 = 0x0483 (SDCC gsinit_startup after the vector table, tier
#     'startup-inf') + 15 vector-table LJMP opcodes (0x02: 12 app IRQ + col133 post-table + INT1(21)/
#     v15(125) boot-redirect, spike §3f / solver) + 28 crib-validated (two hex LUTs, spike §3d).
#   => 110 of the 512 page-bytes are known PER PAGE. The added columns are GLOBAL (one shared
#      keystream), so they decode on EVERY page — but only L=10/L=11 spell the LUTs.
#   Tmr0(13)/Tmr2(45) opcodes stay AMBIGUOUS (LJMP-vs-erased) -> ks_conjectural.json (NOT applied here).
#   Low-confidence guesses live SEPARATELY in firmware/ks_conjectural.json (NOT applied here).
#
# For EACH version there are TWO stacked tables over the same 512 page-bytes:
#   [HEX view]   lowercase hex per byte; unknown byte = 'xx'.
#   [ASCII view] (directly below) 2 places/byte, leading space: ' '+char if printable
#                (0x20..0x7e); ' .' = undefined (mask byte unknown); ' _' = known non-printable.
#   Both use groups of 8 bytes (the 8-byte record structure) and are COLUMN-ALIGNED — a byte in
#   the HEX table sits at the same character column as its ' c' in the ASCII table below. Rows in
#   both tables carry the same PG/L/ADDR prefix, so you match a HEX row to its ASCII row by PG/L.
#
# MASK-K ROW (top of each table): keystream K per column — hex in the HEX table, char in the
#   ASCII table — so K[col] sits above the byte column below. '--'/' .' where K is not recovered.
#   (K is identical for all 3 versions; K[0] CRC unknown, K[1]=0x06 SEQ.)
#
# 2-byte per-sector field (cols 0-1) = CRC + SEQ (spike §1):
#   CRC = col0 = per-page CONTENT checksum (8-bit), body-locked. ENCRYPTED (K[0] unknown)
#         -> shown as RAW CIPHERTEXT, UPPERCASE. true CRC=(CRC-K[0])&0xFF.
#   SEQ = col1 = logical-page field. DECODED: SEQ_plain=(0x20*L) mod255, K[1]=0x06.
#         -> L (0..58) in the L column; 'E' = end-of-image sentinel (L=-1). Sectors are
#         stored PERMUTED in the file; SEQ carries the true flash position.
#
# Notable readable regions (visible in the ASCII view):
#   L=0  @0x0400 : interrupt VECTOR TABLE — reset LJMP (02 04 83) + 15 LJMP opcodes (02) + target-hi
#                  bytes (lattice); 14 app-code + 2 boot-redirect (INT1/v15) + 2 FF-ambiguous (Tmr0/Tmr2).
#   L=10 @0x1800 : hex LUT "0123456789abcdef" at page offset 205.
#   L=11 @0x1A00 : hex LUT "0123456789ABCDEF" at page offset 437.
#
# Row prefix (rows SORTED BY ADDR; sentinel 'E' last):
#   PG=orig .ofw sector 0..59 (permuted) · L=logical page (from SEQ) · ADDR=0x0400+L*0x200 ·
#   CLASS=ff@lat(>90%)/ff-mix(>50%)/data (fraction of the 64 LATTICE cols that are 0xFF; NOT a
#   blank page — no page is fully 0xFF) · CRC=UPPERCASE raw col0 · SEQ=UPPERCASE raw col1.
#
# Rows are wide (~1090 chars) — view with line-wrap OFF.
#====================================================================================================

### FIRMWARE Uosc123.ofw  (version 1.23)  — 60 sectors x (512B page + 2B field)
#   page classes: {'ff@lat': 12, 'data': 46, 'ff-mix': 2}   (known plaintext bytes/page = 110; unknown = 402)
